2026-10-21T23:24:45.2568279Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:aspire2ap] [build-only] Extend the SM90 MegaMoE host SwapAB rollout to 144 tokens while retaining Prefill 2WG

Why:
Decode currently admits 32 EAGLE requests per DP rank because four verification tokens per request reach the host's 128-token SwapAB cutoff. Raising admission without rebuilding the host extension would send larger batches through the slower non-SwapAB geometry. Prepare the host extension for up to 36 requests per rank without changing the existing Prefill optimization.

What:
Restore sm90-swap-ab-144.patch byte-for-byte from d2fd691. Apply it before the existing sm90-prefill-2wg.patch in the host series. Only the SwapAB rollout cutoff changes from 128 to 144; the Prefill patch remains byte-identical and Decode batches at or below 128 retain the same choices. The installed header series is unchanged; the later serving change carries its geometry guard as an overlay.
Exercise image metadata checks with both the independent two-patch fixture and the actual repository host series. The existing image validator already checks ordered patch names, SHA-256 values, manifest inclusion and the real rebuilt extension, so it needs no implementation change.

Checks:
Both host patches apply in series order to a pristine archive of DeepGEMM v0.1.5, commit 1c8138a59b3df5dac50b76b730834ed57982fdfb, without conflicts or patch edits.
The full csrc/tvm_ffi_api.cpp passes g++ C++17 syntax-only compilation with local torch, tvm_ffi and CUDA 13 headers, with zero errors and only the existing torch C++20-extension warnings.
All 85 image-related tests pass, including the actual repository two-patch series and corruption rejection. The combined build and serving working tree passes all 448 CPU pytest cases and static checks. A temporary runner shortens only socket fixtures through a Linux directory-fd alias to the same X-contained directories; test code and fixture git operations are unchanged. The plain long-path run failed three unrelated Unix-socket cases with AF_UNIX path too long; the handoff report retains both logs.
The restored host patch SHA-256 is 5ddffa4a18f8a3b53f4cd4c40631b4b4bd0ea5f6c58fb297ae2555953b7d01cb. The unchanged Prefill host patch SHA-256 is 6a2f587c0484d956b709d99d97c8349fd512b11c3c37c3f6837e870b1f08f4ee.

Expected:
The CPU build route produces a new Firmus image whose host can choose the existing SwapAB geometry for 129 through 144 tokens per rank. A subsequent serving commit must pin that image before raising admission. This commit alone retains the current serving configuration and the current Prefill 2WG switch.

Risks:
Syntax-only compilation does not link or load the rebuilt extension. The new image build, real image metadata validation, GPU numeric gate, official parser and serving performance remain for the parent session. No GPU work, image build, commit, push or branch creation was performed during preparation. No throughput gain is claimed.

2026-10-22T00:09:47.9380000Z Expanded: (true && ((('push' == 'push') && (contains('[require-build] [cluster:aspire2ap] [build-only] Extend the SM90 MegaMoE host SwapAB rollout to 144 tokens while retaining Prefill 2WG

Why:
Decode currently admits 32 EAGLE requests per DP rank because four verification tokens per request reach the host''s 128-token SwapAB cutoff. Raising admission without rebuilding the host extension would send larger batches through the slower non-SwapAB geometry. Prepare the host extension for up to 36 requests per rank without changing the existing Prefill optimization.

What:
Restore sm90-swap-ab-144.patch byte-for-byte from d2fd691. Apply it before the existing sm90-prefill-2wg.patch in the host series. Only the SwapAB rollout cutoff changes from 128 to 144; the Prefill patch remains byte-identical and Decode batches at or below 128 retain the same choices. The installed header series is unchanged; the later serving change carries its geometry guard as an overlay.
